## `R2a/b/d`'s step list — closed 2026-08-28, kept as written

**Written 2026-08-27 on entering the gate, before any of it was done.** Budget
**猜, uncalibrated: 6 segments**, all desk, none of it needing power. The gate
board's estimate says 5; the plan's §12.1 says 6. Neither is calibrated and the
difference is not worth resolving before the first step reports.

🔴 **What the first hour of this gate refuted, and it was in this file.**
§ Blocked on said on 2026-08-26 that the reproducible-build container gates
`R2c` and that *"`R2a/b/d` needs none of it"*. It does. `plan/router-rebuild-plan.md:1084`
heads that container with **"前置 —— 可重現的建置環境（1 個工作段，`R2a` 之前）"**,
and the reason is in the step definitions: `R2a` **rebuilds BusyBox 1.13.4 with
T-vendor** and `R2b` **builds each of three drops' `boa`**. Both need the 32-bit
`rsdk` running. The narrowing had taken the gate board's *"none of it needs
`R1`"* and read it as *none of it needs the container*; those are two different
sentences about two different things.

**What is actually startable without the container** — and it is most of the
value: the **six-tree similarity matrix**, whose material is already on disk
(量 2026-08-27: six `boa` at 526,732 / 522,556 / 509,632 / **485,012 ← this
unit** / 404,904 / 400,424 bytes, matching the plan's table row for row, and six
`busybox` beside them), and **`R2d`'s two greps**. So the gate opens there, and
the container becomes a step of this gate rather than a wall in front of it.

| | question | `SPEC.md` |
|:-:|---|---|
| ⓐ | which GPL drop this firmware was built from — **decided by a metric with a stated floor**, not by a resemblance | `TC-02` — 🔄 **and `-1` answered half of it**: the floor exists and is stated (`boa unit-2018` against `busybox unit-2018` = 0.1581, the `CROSS` cell itself), the matrix places this unit, and `TC-02` still cannot move off 推 from shipped images alone. `TC-12`, `TC-02a` |
| ⓑ | what the vendor kernel emulates, and with what cache-management model | `CPU-44` 殘留, `C-7`/`F51` |
| ⓒ | a reproducible build environment that can run `rsdk` at all — **and it is `P4a`'s precondition too**, which is why it belongs to a gate rather than to a session | `TC-05` |

| Step | | What it produces | DoD | Where it is most likely to be wrong |
|---|---:|---|---|---|
| **`R2a/b/d-0`** ✅ | desk 1 | **The similarity metric itself, with its controls before its results.** `binsim`, and the `BASE`/`FLOOR` thresholds computed from the six trees rather than chosen | The plan's own failure condition is evaluated and **printed**: *if all fifteen pairwise scores span < 5 percentage points the metric has no discrimination and its numbers are void*. Plus a positive control (a file against itself = 1.0) and a negative one (a file against `/dev/urandom` of the same length) ✅ **2026-08-27**: `tools/binsim.py` (32 controls, `k=7` chosen by a pre-registered rule), `tools/binsim-corpus.tsv`, `tools/test-binsim.sh` (71 cases), `notes/binsim.md`. The failure condition was evaluated and printed: `boa` spans **92.8 pp**, `busybox` **83.5 pp**, `acltd` **0.0 pp** and the void verdict fires there, which is the verdict's own positive control. 🔴 **And the step refuted the plan's `FLOOR`**: 0.0650 against a cross-program floor of 0.1581. | **Size dominates.** The six `boa` run 400,424 to 526,732 bytes and any metric with a length term measures config, not toolchain. 量 2026-08-27, and the second reading is the one that matters: `v2.1.2` and `n300rt-2.1.6`'s `busybox` are the **same size** and **differ in exactly 8 bytes, all of them digits in the BusyBox banner's build date** — 99.997 % identical, one build rebuilt on another day. So `busybox` has **five** independent samples across the six trees, not six; `boa` has six. That pair is the free calibration point: a metric that does not put it at the very top is not measuring structure, and a metric that puts everything there is not measuring anything |
| **`R2a/b/d-1`** ✅ | desk 1 | **The matrix**: fifteen pairwise scores over six real builds, `boa` and `busybox` separately. **Nobody has done this** | Every cell has a number, `BASE` and `FLOOR` are named cells of that matrix, and the two are separated by more than the metric's own noise. 🔴 **2026-08-27 — `-0` changed this DoD before this step started.** The separation is not in doubt: `BASE` 0.9818 against `FLOOR` 0.0650 over a noise floor of 0.0000. 🔄 **That noise floor was refuted the same day it was written** — the pairs it came from are selected *by* the byte-equality of the window they are then scored on, so 1.000 is arithmetic; the estimate is 8.0e-4 and it is the smallest of three candidates. The DoD sentence is left as written because this column is the record of what the step was asked to do. What is in doubt is `FLOOR` itself, which sits **below** `CROSS` = 0.1581, the score of two DIFFERENT PROGRAMS from one tree. So this step's first output is not a reading of the clusters — it is **a named replacement floor at or above `CROSS`**, written into `tools/binsim-corpus.tsv`'s `@floor` row, after which `binsim --corpus` stops exiting 1. Until then the plan's three-band rule has a middle band that carries no information. ✅ **2026-08-27**: `@floor` = `boa unit-2018` against `busybox unit-2018` = **0.1581**, the `CROSS` cell itself, in a five-field cross-program form the manifest grew for it. 🔴 **The step's first answer was `busybox unit-2018 v3.4.0` = 0.1646 and the adversarial review killed it on the denominator**: containment divides by the smaller feature set, busybox's is 1.46× boa's, and at a matched denominator the ordering reverses — a pair sharing its whole upstream source across the model change scores 0.1212, below the 0.1551–0.1581 a pair sharing no source reaches. So at this scale no cell sits above the no-shared-source level and below `BASE`, and the tightest correct floor *is* that level. `E7` asserts it is the top of a three-program population at one denominator; `E8` measures what a model change alone costs, which turns the rule's `VOID` precondition into a reading. `--corpus` exits 0. `notes/which-drop.md` owns the decision, the rejected candidates, and the reading. **`TC-02` stays 推** — the corpus holds six shipped images and none of them is a GPL drop | 🔄 **2026-08-27, and this column was the one that was wrong.** It said *"reading a cluster as a toolchain when it is a product line … date and product are confounded with SDK"*. **Product line is crossed with the clustering, not confounded with it**, and the corpus refutes it on its own: 讀 `/etc/version`, N150RT is in all three clusters, two different products inside one cluster score 0.9863/0.9818, and two builds of one product across clusters score 0.0650. What stayed collinear is date and SDK generation, which is a tautology. The way this step could still have gone wrong is the one it did not: **reading "which cluster" as "which drop"** — six shipped images cannot name a source release, and §5 of the note says so rather than reaching |
| **`R2a/b/d-2`** ✅ | desk 1 | **`R2d`, the two greps** — which instructions the vendor kernel emulates, and its cache-management model. Five minutes of work whose value is that it is *this unit's* kernel | Each grep has a positive control that fires, in the sense `notes/cache-model.md` already had to learn: a scanner that finds nothing in one file must be shown finding something in another | **The zero.** The `cache`-instruction scan already returned a false zero once by being run on `stage2.bin` alone (Corrections, 2026-08-26). The emulation grep is the same shape. 🔴 **And it happened again, twice, in this step** — once when a survey of the board configs reported `ULS=0` for `rtl8196e` because that board has no plain `config.linux-2.6.30`, only five suffixed variants, and `grep -l` on an absent file says nothing; once when the `--mips16` test reported a clean `boa` because `--base 0` was wrong for a binary that loads at `0x00400000`, so its `jal` control was 0/515 in range too. Both were caught by asking why a control was also zero, and both are now cases. ✅ **2026-08-27**: `notes/vendor-kernel-isa.md` (new, the owner), `tools/isa-probe.sh` + `tools/test-isa-probe.sh` (40 cases), `opcount.py --pairs` and `--mips16`, `hazlint` **1.3** (K10, K4b, and a correction to 1.2's own note), `SPEC.md` `CPU-47`/`CPU-48` and nine amended rows, and `CLAUDE.md`'s core-naming ban lifted on the `PRId` table it named as its own condition |
| **`R2a/b/d-3`** ✅ | desk 1 | **The container decision**, made and written down: Docker Desktop's WSL integration / `apt install docker.io` in the distro / `debootstrap` + a pinned `snapshot.debian.org` chroot with no daemon | `rsdk-linux-gcc --version` prints `4.4.5` inside it, and the recipe is in a file rather than in a shell history. 🔴 **2026-08-28 — this DoD was replaced before it was met, because `-2` had already fired it without doing the work.** The replacement is a real build: `tools/tc-smoke.sh`'s four rungs for every toolchain found, and above them a complete `vmlinux`. ✅ Met: three rsdk releases reach L4; `users/dhrystone` builds and runs with all three and every self-check value matches; **724 objects and a linked `vmlinux` from two different toolchains, rc=0 both times**. The recipe is in `notes/vendor-toolchains.md` §3 with pinned versions and sha256s | 量 2026-08-27: the distro has **no `docker`, no `podman`, no `debootstrap`**; the only `docker` on `PATH` is Docker Desktop's `/mnt/c` shim, which `LOG.md` 2026-08-24 records reporting *could not be found in this WSL 2 distro*. Route ① is the one already measured not to work. 🔴 **2026-08-27, from `-2`: the whole premise is refuted.** Both rsdk toolchains run natively in this distro — `rsdk-1.3.6-4181`'s gcc prints `3.4.6-1.3.6` and `rsdk-1.5.5-5281`'s prints `4.4.5-1.5.5p4`, exit 0, no container — so **this step's own DoD signal fired without any of its three routes**. What is left is not a decision: `rsdk-1.5.5`'s `as` misses exactly one 32-bit library, i386 `libz.so.1`, with `/lib32/libc.so.6` and `/lib/ld-linux.so.2` already present. **The step is not ticked**, because a step whose premise was refuted has not been performed; it is reshaped, and the residual is named rather than left as a wall 🔴 **2026-08-28: two of the three ways it could have been wrong, were.** The residual was recorded as *one* missing library for *`as`*; it is **seventeen binutils programs**, and the reason `--version` looked fine is that gcc/cpp/xgcc are statically linked. And the step was framed as supplying a dependency; the thing it actually produced is `TC-15` — the vendor's own compiler and assembler agreeing on which Lexra cores expose the load delay slot, and the measured cost of building this 4181 board with the 5281-configured rsdk. ⚠️ **Not proved**: the `vmlinux` built here has never been run, on the device or in an emulator |
| **`R2a/b/d-4`** ✅ | desk 2 | **`R2a` and `R2b` proper**: rebuild BusyBox 1.13.4 and three drops' `boa`, score them against this unit's binaries with the `-0` metric | `binsim(rebuild, unit-2018) >= BASE` for a named drop, or an explicit *undetermined* with the score. ✅ **2026-08-28: the second branch, with the score and with the reason.** Ten cells built (3 drops × 3 rsdk, 7 of 9, plus a synthesised 1.5.5-at-`-march=4181`); best is **0.8255**, which is **warn**, not pass. 🔴 **And the DoD's own premise did not survive the step**: `TC-18` measures a `-march` change at 0.3360 and a total source replacement at 0.9359, so this channel identifies the toolchain and not the drop. `TC-02` stays 推 — and now against a closed door rather than an unopened one, because config is measured at ≤ 0.065 against a 0.156 gap | **Comparing my rebuild against my own source proves nothing.** The comparand must be the binary cut out of **this machine's flash dump** — the plan says so in a box and it is the easiest step in this gate to get wrong. ✅ Honoured: `tools/rebuild-census.py` refuses rather than substituting a comparand, and `A6` is the case that pins it. 🔴 **The way it actually went wrong was one level down and it is recorded**: the first harness removed only the final artefacts, so a run that had failed **with the wrong flags** left its `.o` behind and the next run linked them. Every build now re-stages from `src-vendor/`; 389 objects `file`-checked, 0 not big-endian MIPS |

### Refutation condition, written now

> **否證 ⓐ** — the metric's own null: if the fifteen pairwise scores span less
> than 5 percentage points, **no drop is identified and `TC-02` stays 推**. The
> number that would prove the identification wrong is a `BASE` that a *different*
> drop also clears; the matrix is what makes that visible instead of invisible.

🔴 **2026-08-27, `-1`: that condition did NOT fire, and `TC-02` stays 推 anyway.**
The span is 92.8 pp, twenty times the bar. Recorded here rather than quietly
skipped past, because a refutation condition that is satisfied and still leaves
the answer undetermined is saying that it was not the binding constraint. The
binding one was never written down and is now: **a similarity matrix over six
shipped images cannot name a source-and-toolchain release, whatever it scores.**
The condition above is about the *instrument*; `TC-02` is about the *material*.
`notes/which-drop.md` §5.

> **否證 ⓑ** — a grep returning zero is a claim. It is refuted by the same
> scanner finding a hit in a second artefact, and if it cannot, the zero is
> recorded as *not looked for* rather than as *not there*.

### Stop-loss, written now

| If | then |
|---|---|
| The metric has no discrimination after two attempts | `TC-02` stays **推**, and `R3` proceeds on the drop it already uses. **This gate blocks nothing** — that is on the gate board and it is why this is a stop-loss rather than a crisis |
| All three container routes fail | `R2a`/`R2b`'s rebuild halves are recorded **未定**, the matrix and `R2d` still stand on their own, and the container moves to `P4a` where it is load-bearing rather than convenient |

## `R1-gate`'s step list — closed 2026-08-26, kept as written

**Kept in place rather than deleted**, because the column *"where it is most
likely to be wrong"* is only worth anything if it can be read afterwards against
what actually went wrong. **Two of its five rows called it and two did not**:
`R1g-1` predicted in writing that a write-through D-cache would make cells 2 and
3 agree and that this would be *"unnecessary rather than wrong — a result, and it
has to be written as one"*, and that is what happened. `R1g-5`'s row said *"a
write-up is where a gate quietly widens its own claim"*, and the write-up found
two claims already widened. **What no row anticipated** is that the write-up would refute the gate's own reading of `probe1` — the
write-through sentence — and that the refutation would come from a directory
nobody had looked in.

**Written 2026-08-25 on entering the gate, before any of it was done**
(`plan/SESSIONS.md` §0b). `R1-gate` is `R1d` — the cache-management model, on
silicon — and `R1e` — the CP0 census. Budget **8 segments**: 4 desk, 2 bench,
2 instrument (`plan/router-rebuild-plan.md` §12.1). The steps below add to
**8½**, and the half over budget is `R1g-0`, whose question was not visible
until a payload was being designed against it.

**This list, and not `plan/DAY-ZERO.md`, is what "what do I do next at the desk"
means from here.** `DAY-ZERO` owns the instruments that had to exist before any
gate could run; a gate owns its own steps. That boundary is the one item 7
already drew — `hazlint` was `DAY-ZERO`'s, the hazard verdicts it enables are
`R1b`'s — and item 8 had never been cut along it.

| Step | | What it produces | DoD | Where it is most likely to be wrong |
|---|---:|---|---|---|
| **`R1g-0`** | desk ½ | **What a fault costs.** A static read of this unit's own exception path: after `Undefined Exception happen.` and `cp0_cause=%X, cp0_epc=%X`, does control reach the `<RealTek>` prompt, spin, or reset? Is `Status.BEV` 0 or 1 while the loader runs? Does the loader re-install its vectors on a warm reset, or once from ROM? | Each of the three answered with an address, or recorded undetermined with the experiment that decides it | It may not be decidable statically — 🔄 *(that clause named the wrong table — `0x8040A5C0` is `BootStateEvent[3][8]`; the real one is `exception_handlers[32]` at `0x8040EB40`, and thirty of its thirty-two entries DO share a tail. The step's risk was still real, and it is left here as written rather than rewritten to look prescient.)* If it is undetermined, **every later step has to assume a fault costs one power cycle**, which is what puts `R1g-1` ahead of `R1g-2` |
| **`R1g-1`** | desk + instr 2 | **`probe1` — the cache-model discriminator, and the qemu harness.** Six cells, each writing a known instruction into RAM, executing it, and reporting which of two constants came back: ① no flush at all ② `CCTL = 0x002` alone (invalidate I) ③ the vendor's `0x200` then `0x002` (flush D, then invalidate I) ④ the `Status.IsC`/`SwC` path `c-r3k.c` uses and this bootcode never does ⑤ a store through KSEG1 with no flush ⑥ `r3k_cache_size()` reproduced, for I-size, D-size and line size (`CPU-25`). Every cell writes its result to a block at `0x80A00000` **before the next cell starts** | Builds under the `hazlint` gate; runs to its own end under `qemu-system-mips`; the result block is recoverable with one `DW 80A00000` after the payload's watchdog reset, so a hang costs the cells after it and not the cells before it | **Cell ① is the negative control and it is the one that can quietly not work.** If the primed line is evicted between priming and the rewrite, ① reports *not stale* for a reason that has nothing to do with the cache model, and every later cell then looks like a pass. Second: `mtc0 $t,$20` is Lexra's, and three of the six command values `notes/cache-model.md` lists have exactly one source — if it faults, `R1g-0`'s answer decides what that costs. Third: a write-through D-cache makes ② and ③ agree, so the vendor's D-then-I sequence would be **unnecessary rather than wrong** — a result, and it has to be written as one |
| **`R1g-2`** | desk + instr 2 | **`probe2` — the exception handler and the CP0 census.** Install handlers at `0x80000080` **and** `0x80000000` through KSEG1, flushed by whichever method `R1g-1` measured (a build knob, not a constant); then read CP0 registers 0–31 across selects 0–7 and record each as *read / trapped / read as zero*. `Count`(9) and `Compare`(11) read twice around a loop of known length — `F50b`, and it decides three things at once | The full table comes out under qemu; **removing the reserved-encoding control makes the payload refuse to emit a table at all**; the handler's installation is self-checking, so *the handler did not take* and *the instruction is absent* are different observations | `Status.BEV` at the prompt is `CPU-27` and it is **blank** — if it is 1 the vectors are in boot ROM and `0x80000080` is the wrong address entirely. 🔴 **And the address itself was wrong in four files until 2026-08-25**: `0x80000180` is MIPS32's; this core is R3000-class, so `0x80000000` is the UTLB refill vector and `0x80000080` is the general one, and a handler at `0x180` would have looked installed and changed nothing. The payload has to read `BEV` and refuse rather than install blind. Second: overwriting `0x80000180` may leave the loader without a handler after the watchdog reset — `R1g-0` question 4. Third: reading a CP0 register that does not exist is architecturally UNDEFINED, not a trap; a core that returns stale bus data would make *read as zero* and *not implemented* indistinguishable, and the census has to say which of the three it can actually tell apart |
| **`R1g-3`** | desk 1 | **The seating sheet.** A new `RUNSHEET.md` section: every cell's expected value and refutation condition written **before** power, the do-not-type list, and the ride-alongs that cost nothing once the board is up — `C-17`'s `J BFC00000` then `DW 81000400 16`, `NET-13`'s four cable moves with the jack in the `--out` filename, and `CLK-08b` on the 2 ms ESC grid | `tools/check-predictions.py` passes on a prediction block written before the first capture, as it did for all 18 blocks of seating 2 | The failure mode has already happened twice and both times it was an expected value derived from the wrong power cycle (`D2c`) or from the very map the cell was supposed to test (`E10d`). **Every expected value has to name the capture it came from** |
| **`R1g-4a`** ✅ | bench 1 | 🔴 **DONE 2026-08-25, and its DoD was met on both halves.** `probe1`'s block came back on the UART **and** from `0x80A00000`, and the two agree **104/104 row words** with a one-word shift as the negative control; cell 1 read against cell 5 on the `ma` column returned `240222b2`/`240222b2` — **the D-cache is write-through**, which is the write-back-vs-write-through measurement the DoD asked for. The known defect carried in knowingly **fired exactly where it was predicted to**: cell 4 is `07` CORRUPT on both victims, `rlx_isc_inv`'s `sb $0` reached DRAM, and rows 0–3 were banked first, so the negative control and the discriminator survived it. `RUNSHEET.md` § Results B4. *(Original text:)* **The first seating, and it stops before `probe2`.** `H0`'s six free reads, `probe1`, read back, then the three ride-alongs. 🔄 **Split from `R1g-4` on 2026-08-25** after `probe1.c`/`probe2.c` were audited — `docs/rlxprobe-audit-2026-08-25.md`. `rlx_reset` hands the prompt back by itself, so ending here spends nothing extra this visit | `probe1`'s result block recovered from the UART **and** from `0x80A00000`, and the two agree; cell 1 read against cell 5 on the `ma` column, which is a `write-back`-vs-`write-through` measurement in its own right | `MEM-15`: this DRAM keeps its **contents** across a short power-off, so a result block from the previous payload can be read as this one's. Every block needs a per-build nonce and a per-cell written flag, and the region must be poisoned on entry — otherwise *cell 5 never ran* and *cell 5 returned 0* are the same bytes. 🆕 **And one known defect is carried in knowingly**: `CELLS[]` runs cell 4 — the only cell with a demonstrated kill — third. Carried because rows 0–3 are banked first and because `rlx_isc_inv` enters with a safe `$a0`, so a fault there **prints**. That is the exact property `rlx_do_break` lacks, and it is the line the split was decided on |
| **`R1g-4b`** ✅ | desk 1 + bench 1 | 🔴 **DONE 2026-08-25b, and its DoD was met on all three legs.** `probe2`'s block came back on the UART **and** from `0x80A01000`, and the two agree on all 40 header words, on the seal and on every spot-checked census row. **The `break` control failed visibly or not at all — and it did not fail**: `install.bad=0`, `break.count=1`, `cause=00000024`, `epc=80500270`. And `p2a`/`p2b` no longer exist as a pair. 🔴 **The one thing the DoD did not ask for and should have**: a repeatability control. The attempt at one booted the vendor kernel, because **the loader re-stages `0x80500000` on a watchdog reset** — a structural fact about every future seating, now recorded. `RUNSHEET.md` § Results B4 `R1g-4b`. *(Original text:)* **`probe2`, after it is fixed.** The must-fix list is `docs/rlxprobe-audit-2026-08-25.md` § Must-fix: `SAFE_A0` on `rlx_do_break`; a vector read-back in `install_handler`; `$v0`/`$8`/`$15` sentinels and the `exc_rec` bracket on `rlx_count_delta`; `V_DIRTY` in `probe1`. ~24 lines, and **the re-validation is the cost, not the lines** — qemu, the 66-case suite, and a mutation per fix, because a suite that cannot tell the patched payload from the shipped one is `hazlint` 1.0's finding 6 | `probe2`'s block recovered on both channels; **the `break` control fails visibly or not at all**; and `p2a`/`p2b` no longer exist as a pair, because `R1g-4a`'s `H1` decided the flush | **This costs one additional planned power cycle and that is the whole price of the split.** What it buys: `H0b` measures `exception_handlers[9]` — the single unverified link in the `SAFE_A0` finding — `H0c` measures what a kuseg fault lands on, `H0a3` measures whether the vector page is coherent uncached, and `H1` collapses two binaries into one. **The fix gets written against measured values instead of read ones** |
| **`R1g-5`** ✅ | desk 1 | 🔴 **DONE 2026-08-26, and its own risk column called it.** `docs/rlx-cache-and-cp0.md` written; `CPU-19` upgraded to **量** — and the upgrade is not the one this row anticipated: what became measured is the **classification** (`Config.M = 0`, the select field ignored, `rfe` balancing the R3000 stack, `Random` inside 0…31 — four routes, no TLB probe), not only the mechanism. `C-6`'s residual restated and **re-homed to `R1h`**, because both of its owning gates closed under it. 🔴 **The row said *"a write-up is where a gate quietly widens its own claim"*, and the write-up found two claims already widened**: *"the D-cache is write-through"* (`probe1`'s two cells both stored to a line the D-cache did not hold, so write-through and write-back-without-write-allocate give the same reading — and both GPL drops' `boards/rtl8196e` say `CONFIG_ARCH_CACHE_WBC=y`), and *"no `cache` instruction in vendor code that executes"* (**37 of them in this unit's own kernel**, D side only, `0x8000CA40`–`0x8000CD4C` — the scan that returned zero had been run on `stage2.bin` alone). Decision ① is **narrowed, not answered**: the instruction side is 量, the flash-read-back side is decision ②'s question. `SPEC.md` `CPU-19`/`CPU-20`–`CPU-24`/`CPU-25`/`CPU-43`–`CPU-45`; `tools/spec-check.py` gained **C8** and a ninth mutation after `CPU-19` turned out to have been outside two checks since the day it was written. *(Original text:)* **The write-up.** `docs/rlx-cache-and-cp0.md`; `SPEC.md` `CPU-04`, `CPU-25`, `CPU-27`, the `CCTL` command rows, `CPU-19` upgraded from *read* to *measured*; `C-6` closes or its residual is restated; and the four downstream decisions this gate exists to unblock are recorded with the reading that decided each | `tools/spec-check.py` clean, and each of the four decisions names a measurement rather than an argument. 🔴 **Met on three of four, and the fourth is recorded as not met**: decision ② names **no** measurement, because there is none — it names the experiment instead, which is this project's own stated form for *uncertain* (`CLAUDE.md`). **The gate closed on that, deliberately, and the discrepancy is written into the closing statement rather than smoothed over** | A write-up is where a gate quietly widens its own claim. `R1d` measures **which flush works on this core**; it does not measure why, and it licenses no statement about the Lexra family |

**The four decisions `R1-gate` exists to unblock**, so that closing it can be
checked against something: ① where `R5b`'s MTD driver has to flush, ② whether
`R6`'s descriptor rings need an uncached window or a flush, ③ whether the
exception handler `R1a` needs can live at `0x80000080`, ④ whether `R5-0`'s SoC
timer driver is a **prerequisite** or a bonus (`F50b` — if `Count`/`Compare` are
absent it is a prerequisite, and `R1c`'s timing method loses its first route).

🔴 **How they came out, 2026-08-26. Three named a measurement; one did not, and
the gate closed anyway — on purpose.** The full statement with its evidence is
`docs/rlx-cache-and-cp0.md`; this is the scoreboard.

| | verdict | what decided it |
|:-:|---|---|
| ① | 🔄 **answered for the instruction side, and narrowed there** | `CCTL 0x002` alone, 量 twice on independent ground (`probe1` cells 2/3/6; `probe2`'s handler install through KSEG1). **An MTD driver also reads flash back**, and a window whose contents changed underneath the D-cache is decision ②'s question — so half of ① travels with ② |
| ② | 🔴 **未答** | Nothing. `R1d` measured the CPU→memory direction **for a write miss only**, and nothing at all in the memory→CPU direction. `R1h` cells A–E decide it |
| ③ | 🔴 **yes** | `Status = 1000fc00`, bit 22 = 0; `break.count = 1`, `cause = 00000024`, `epc = 80500270`, and the handler restored with `restore.mismatch = 0` |
| ④ | 🔴 **prerequisite** | row `0x48` = `00000000` `S_ZERO`, `count.delta = 0` over 100,000 iterations, and `nowrite = 0` over 256 rows is what makes that zero a real zero |

**Why closing with ② open is the right move rather than the lenient one.** What
② is missing is not more analysis of `R1d`'s captures — it is **a payload that
does not exist and a seating that is not scheduled until `R3`**. A gate held open
waiting for a future seating is a backlog item wearing a gate's name; `R1h` gives
② an owner, a payload, a DoD and a stop-loss. **And the price of closing is paid
in ①**: it is recorded as narrowed rather than answered, which is the sentence a
driver author needs and the one a clean-looking gate would have lost.

### Preconditions — checked, and two are not satisfied

| | |
|---|---|
| ✅ | **`R0` closed**, so the transport is proved: `rescue` + `put` + `J`, with `AUTOBURN` measured `00000000` at the burn path's own instruction *during* the transfers |
| ✅ | **`hazlint` 1.1 exists and gates the build** — `probe0.bin` depends on `probe0.gate`, which does not exist unless `hazlint` exited 0 |
| ✅ | **The `rlxprobe` chassis exists** — segment 0, 23/23, and `-march=mips1 -msoft-float` is the measured build line for gcc 12.4.0 (`TC-06`) |
| ✅ | **`qemu-system-mips` 8.2.2 is installed** |
| 🔄 | **What a fault costs — answered statically by `R1g-0`, and three of its inputs are now measured.** `do_reserved` at `0x80400BE8` prints twice and branches to itself with `IEc` 0 and the watchdog unarmed, so **a fault the loader does not handle costs one power cycle**. 2026-08-25 at the bench: `exception_handlers` **is** at `0x8040EB40` with thirty of thirty-two entries `= 80400BE8`, **including index 9** (`H0b`); the general vector at `0x80000080` holds the dispatcher, identically through the cached and uncached windows (`H0a`/`H0a2`/`H0a3`); and the UTLB refill vector holds `5A5AA5A5`, opcode 22 `BLEZL` (`H0c`) — **not `j` and not `jal`**, so `cache.S`'s no-demonstrated-brick-path argument holds by measurement. **`probe2` is still designed for *a fault ends the seating*, and that is now a cost that has been priced rather than assumed.** ⚠️ Not measured: whether the core **fetches** from `0x80000080`. That is `Status.BEV` |
| 🔄 | **`Status.BEV` at the prompt: upgraded from *blank* to *讀, one source*, at the desk on 2026-08-25, and the evidence was already in this repository.** `docs/loader-phy-and-switch.md` §2's interrupt-layer table has carried *"`Status.IM[7:2]` unmasked, **`BEV` cleared** — set at `0x80406694`, called from `0x80408634`, on the boot path, **never re-masked**"* since 2026-08-23, while `SPEC.md` `CPU-27` and this table both said *not established*. **A fact with one owner that never reached the two files that depend on it** — the same defect class as `43ec0e0`. It is *讀* and not *量*: the tick counter advancing at 100.0018 Hz (`REG-25`, 量) proves an interrupt is being **serviced**, but a `BEV=1` machine servicing it through the ROM vector produces the same reading, so the chain does not isolate `BEV`. **No loader command can read CP0**, so the measurement needs a payload — and with `H2` deferred to `R1g-4b`, `CPU-27` stays *讀* through `R1g-4a`. `probe2` reads it itself and refuses to install if it is not 0, which is the refutation condition |

### Carried forward owned by this gate

🔴 **`C-6` → `R1d`, `R1e` — and both of its owning gates closed under it, which by
this list's own rule makes it the second orphan.** **Re-homed to `R1h`,
2026-08-26**, the same day `R1-gate` closed. *(The precedent is `C-16` below. A
list whose rule is "an item with no owning gate is a bug" has to treat "an item
whose gate closed without it" the same way — and this is the first time that rule
was applied on the day rather than noticed a gate later.)*

**Closed on silicon**: the flush recipe for the **instruction** side (`CCTL
0x002` alone), `Status.IsC` not isolating, and CP0 20's read side — it reads `0`
for real, because `nowrite = 0` over 256 rows proves `mfc0` always writes `rt`
(`CPU-39`), so **CP0 20 is a write-only command register that reads back zero**.

🆕 **Closed at the desk on 2026-08-26, and not by the route this row named**:
**four of the seven CCTL commands now have a name from a source that states
it** — `arch/rlx/mm/cache-rlx.c` gives `0x1 DInval`, `0x2 IInval`, `0x100 DWB`,
`0x200 DWB_Inval` — and `0x100` is a command this repository had no row for at
all. This row used to say the residual needed *"R1e or a `devmem`-class read"*.
**`R1e` ran and could not do it**: the census reads CP0 20 and it reads zero, and
**a read side that is always zero cannot name a write side.** The route that
worked was a directory nobody had opened.

🔴 **Still open, itemised, and each with an owner** — all of them `R1h`:
~~`CCTL 0x010`/`0x020`, unnamed in every source~~ 🔴 **CLOSED 2026-08-26 at the desk**: `0x010 = IMEM0FILL`, `0x020 = IMEM0OFF`, four sources, and `probe3` writes `0x020` as the I-MEM discriminator. **Residual is `CPU-24` 殘留** — bits 6/7 and `0x400`/`0x800`, where the sources **contradict each other** rather than being silent. *(as written:)* (`CPU-24`, **and only its
documentary half**: writing an unnamed command to a cache controller is
declined); cache size / line size / associativity (`CPU-25`, which now has a
**prediction to refute** rather than a blank); the **D side of coherence**
(`CPU-45` — this is `R1-gate`'s decision ②); whether the `cache` instruction
retires on this silicon (`CPU-44`); and write-through versus
write-back-without-write-allocate, together with whether `Status.IsC`/`SwC` exist
as bits (`CPU-19` 殘留). *(Original text:)* Answered at the desk as the R3000
model; its residual
is what this gate measures — `CCTL` commands `0x010` and `0x020` have one source
each and no name in any source, and cache size, line size and associativity are
unknown. `CPU-25` and `CPU-27` are the `SPEC.md` blanks that close with it, and
`CPU-04` closes with `R1e`.

🔴 **And one item is orphaned, which is a defect in that list by its own rule.**
`C-16` — *what actually copies flash `0x060010` into RAM at `0x80500000`* — names
`R0` as its owning gate, and **`R0` closed on 2026-08-24 with `C-16` still open**.
A list whose rule is *an item with no owning gate is a bug* has to treat *an item
whose gate closed without it* the same way.

### Refutation condition, verbatim

> **否證** — 任一負控制沒觸發 → 整張表作廢。**這是唯一能讓這張表變垃圾的失敗，
> 所以它是強制的** (`plan/router-rebuild-plan.md` §6-R1 整體驗收)

> **否證** — 兩個來源不一致 → 記「未確定」，並且**在裸機上用一個自我修改的實驗
> 判決**（寫一條指令進 RAM，刷，跳進去，看執行到的是新的還是舊的） (§6-R1d)

For this gate that reads: **if `probe1` cell ① reports that the freshly written
instruction executes with no flush at all, the other five cells decide nothing**,
because a test that passes without the treatment has not tested the treatment.
The gate does not then close with *"any flush works"*; it closes with *cell ① did
not hold* and a redesign.

### Stop-loss, written now

**Written before the work, because a stop-loss written after three segments have
been spent is a stop-loss nobody applies.**

| If | then |
|---|---|
| **Two seatings** pass and `probe1` cell ① still cannot be made to hold | `R1d` is recorded **未定**. `R5b` and `R6` then carry the conservative cost — flush D-then-I through `CCTL` **and** take the `Status.IsC` path — and the gate closes on that, not on a model |
| `Status.BEV` reads 1, or the handler cannot be installed | The census falls back to the registers that can be read without one, the rest is **未定**, and `F50b` resolves against `Count`/`Compare` — i.e. **`R5-0`'s timer driver becomes a prerequisite**, which is a decision and not a gap |
| `mtc0 $t,$20` faults | `CCTL` is recorded as not reachable from a payload in this state, the `Status.IsC` path becomes the only route, and `notes/cache-model.md`'s two-mechanism reading is **half refuted on silicon** — which is worth more than the gate's own answer |
| Three seatings spent with none of the four downstream decisions settled | Stop. `R1-gate` is not blocking `R3` — the v6 ladder already says so — and the honest move is to run `R3` on the vendor's own cache handling and come back |

**And the gate-crossing rule applies on the way out** (§18.3): one entry in
`study/weekly-results.md` — a one-line version, three claims that stand each with
its evidence, and **what this gate did not prove**. Two consecutive entries whose
*did not prove* is the same thing make that thing the next gate.

🔄 **198 → 200 on 2026-09-01**, and the ORDER matters: the number was written as 198+2 and then CHECKED by summing the column, which is backwards and is recorded that way. The sum is **200 over 18 rows**, with `R1h` deliberately uncosted (its budget lives in its own step list). The paragraphs below say why this column is not a quantity; that is a reason to delete it, not a reason to let its arithmetic drift.

**Est. is uncalibrated.** The calibration point is the first driver (R5). When it
lands, multiply every remaining row by the measured ratio and say so here.

🔴 **This column had a second owner, and reconciling the two on 2026-08-25
produced a worse answer than "they disagree": neither of them is a quantity.**

The planning material carries a finer accounting — desk, bench and instrument
time as three columns — and totals **252**. This column totals **198**. Both add
up correctly against themselves: every row of the plan's table equals its own
desk+bench+instrument, its cumulative column is consistent, and the seventeen
rows here sum to 198. **So the arithmetic was never the problem.**

**What the difference is not.** The plan's instrument column totals 44, so
252 − 44 = **208** is its desk+bench. This column is 198, not 208. Ten of its
seventeen rows agree with the plan's desk+bench exactly; **seven disagree, and
not all in the same direction** — `R5` is 4 low, `R1-pub` 2 low, `R7` is 1
**high**. Net −10.

🔴 **So 198 is not the total, not desk+bench, and not any consistent subset of
the plan. No rule reproduces it.** It is not a stale copy either: a stale copy
names a revision you can point at. It is a third number that accreted row by row.

**Which one a decision should be made against, and why it is the plan's.** The
column beside this one is `Actual`, and `Actual` counts segments consumed in
`LOG.md` between one gate closing and the next — **which includes instrument
days**. The only estimate with the same definition is the plan's 小計. Against
the two closed gates: `S0` actual 1 against plan 3, `R0` actual 7 against plan
11 — **8 consumed against 14 planned, 0.57×, n=2**, and that ratio is the thing
worth carrying rather than either total. Against this column (1 and 5) the same
two gates read 1.0× and 1.4×, which looks better and means less: it is a number
compared against a number with no definition.

**The house-rule-1 defect is sharper than two owners.** An estimate is a planning
artefact; this file is the owner of *where I am*, which is a fact about work
done. `Actual`, `Status` and `Evidence` belong here. **`Est.` does not**, and the
recommended repair is to delete the column rather than reconcile it, keeping one
line under the board for the measured ratio — which IS a fact about work done and
which is what "multiply every remaining row by the measured ratio" needs. **Not
done today**, because deleting a column from the gate board is a decision and
this entry is the analysis it should be made from.

### 🟢 `P4b-1`: the second owner is gone, 2026-09-01 — and the column that was NOT flagged was also a copy

**What this table looked like until today**: four columns — `version`,
`Contents`, `Target (optimistic / ×1.8)`, `Shipped` — of which only `Shipped`
was ever this file's own. The other two restated `plan/CHARTER.md` §88, which
is **gitignored**, so house rule 1's usual repair (replace the content with a
pointer) produced a pointer no public reader could follow. `P4b-1` decided the
owner has to be a committed file, and it is `README.md`.

**量 2026-08-31 (twentieth), the `Contents` column, read side by side against
CHARTER §88 — six of six shared rows disagreed:**

| version | `CHARTER.md` §88 | the deleted `Contents` column |
|---|---|---|
| v0.1 | `R0` + `rlxprobe` segment 0 on silicon + **`R1-gate`** | S0 + R0 + **`R1`** + `rlxprobe` + first write-up |
| v0.2 | `R3` (60-second take) + **`P4a`** | **`R2`** + `R3` |
| v0.3 | `R4` + `R5`, **six** drivers + DT binding + `driver-diff` | `R4` + `R5`, **five** drivers |
| v0.4 | **`R1-pub`** + `P1` | **`R6`** |
| v0.5 | **`R6`** + `P2` | **`R7`** |
| v1.0 | `R8` + `R9` + `P3`/`P4b` | `R8` + `R9` + **`P1`–`P4`** |

CHARTER also has `v0.0` and `v0.6` rows; that table had neither, which is what
shifted its tail by one version from `v0.4` down. **It bit twice in one
morning.** Read there, `v0.2` did not need `P4a` and could have been tagged
before that session started; and `v0.1` needed `R1` **whole**, of which
`R1-pub` is `·`, so under that table v0.1 could never be tagged at all —
while `REL-2` says its contents completed on 2026-08-26, which is true under
CHARTER and false under the file `REL-2` lives in.

🔴 **量 2026-09-01 (twenty-second): the `Target` column was a restatement
too, and nothing had ever said so.** The note above singled out `Contents` as
*the* wrong column and left `Target` beside it unflagged. But `×1.8` is
CHARTER's own multiplier, defined in CHARTER, so that column was never this
file's quantity either. Read against §88 by version label:

| version | CHARTER §88 樂觀 / 務實 | the deleted `Target` column |
|---|---|---|
| v0.1 | 2.5 / 4.5 週 | wk 3 / wk 5 — agrees under rounding |
| v0.2 | 4.9 / 8.9 | **wk 7 / wk 12** |

| v0.5 | 18.5 / 33.3 | **wk 23 / wk 41** |

**Two of six agree and four do not** — and a partly-agreeing copy is worse
than a wholly stale one, because it reads as maintained. ⚠️ The `v0.4`
comparison is by version *label* only: the two tables put different work under
that label, so those two cells were never measuring the same thing.

**Why both columns were deleted rather than reconciled.** The gate board's
`Est.` column analysis below already made this argument and this is the second
instance of it: *an estimate is a planning artefact; this file is the owner of
where I am, which is a fact about work done.* `Shipped` is a fact about work
done and stays. `Target` is an estimate and goes to `plan/`. `Contents` is a
decision about the future, it has to be visible to a public reader, and it goes
to `README.md`. 🔴 **And `CHANGELOG.md`'s `v0.2` section carried the dangling
pointer into the release itself** — its first line read *Contents, against
`plan/CHARTER.md` §88* — which is the defect this gate exists for, shipped.
Repaired in the same commit.

## Session ladder — the rows that are history, kept verbatim

🔄 **2026-09-01 (twenty-third segment): § Now kept three of these and this
section took the other 35.** This file's own header says it owns *current
position*; a session from 2026-08-24 is not current position, so by the
file's own definition those rows were never its state — `LADDER-1`. They
are moved rather than deleted because nothing verified that every sentence
in them also appears in `LOG.md`, and deleting on that assumption is the
move this project does not make.

🔴 **The labels below are FROZEN as of 2026-09-01 and are not updated.**
The recurring cost was never the rows — it was that *"Last session but
twenty-seven"* has to be retyped every segment, which is a label about the
repository's own contents kept in a second place and invalidated by a
change somewhere else: `CNT-1`'s class, thirty-eight times per segment. It
has already produced one recorded bug, the eighth segment entered twice,
found 2026-08-30. **`LOG.md` is the ordered record**; these are its
summaries, in order, with the labels they had on the day they stopped
moving.

**The rule from here**: a new session prepends one row to § Now, and the
row it displaces is moved to the TOP of this section with the label it had
at that moment. Three labels change per segment instead of thirty-eight,
and nothing below this line is ever retyped.

1×**：stage 1 的複製迴圈（`0xBFC001BC`）與 kernel 的 `memcpy`（`0x80002720`）**都是 32 位元 `lw`**。🟢 **而那段反組譯自己重新導出了 20,924**（`22188 − 1264`，兩個立即數），這是本 repo 第一次能給出這個數的來歷。取而代之的兩項都不在候選清單上：**≤9×**，stage 1 的迴圈自己跑在 `0xBFC001D0`（KSEG1，依架構未快取），每圈 8 個指令提取本身就是對它正在讀的那顆 SPI 的讀取 —— 每 4 位元組 **9 次** SPI 字讀對上 kernel 的 **1 次**；**4×**，stage 1 全部 4,848 位元組寫 `SFCR` **0 次**而 stage 2 寫 **2 次**，所以 stage 1 跑在重置預設 `SPI_CLK_DIV = 111B`（DIV 16）上，之後都跑在 `REG-13` 量到的 `001B`（DIV 4）。**1 × 4 × ≤9 = ≤36×，量到 ~16× —— 模型高估 2.1–2.3×，而那不是缺陷是模型沒有的一項**（量到的數是 `busybox wc` 的端到端速率，模型只算匯流排）。⚠️ **重置預設只有一種來源（datasheet），而這個專案從來沒有在 stage 2 執行之前讀過 `SFCR`。** 下一次上機有一格不花電源循環就能判它，三個結果各對應一個判決，寫在 `notes/kernel-build.md` §19.7.4。 🔴 **擁有者稽核抓到一句已經不再為真的話**：`RUNSHEET.md` seating 6 的 Deviation 2 寫著那個 echo 閘*「is not yet an 27）。改了九個檔：`tools/flrbracket.py`、`tools/test-flrbracket-mutants.py`、`ci-expected.tsv`、`ci.yml`、`SPEC.md`（`FW-34`／§17／`REG-13`／`FLS-20`）、`notes/kernel-build.md` rows prefixed `Q-*` because `P0`…`Pn` already means three different things here); `P7` run — sha256 `1a0725c0…`, 29,088 bytes, **byte-identical to `R1h-1`'s 2026-08-26 value**, `hazlint` 0 violations in 804 loads, `flags=50010002` derived from the Makefile's own expression and reproducing the qemu capture's `50070002`; —— 那是 **COP1X 的**運算元模型，`mtc3` 的 rs 欄是 4 **因為 4 是 MT 選擇碼**，舊版把那個 4 當成 `$a0` 讀，所以 `lw a0` 接 `mtc3 t0,$0` 會被報成一個不存在的違規；`control_flow` 不知道 `bc3` 是分支，delay slot 裡的 load 只查得到 fall-through 一條腿 —— **和 `M8` 15。舊的 `reads()` 回 `{rs, rt}`，於是工具把一個**子運算碼選擇欄**當成剛被載入的暫存器，報了一個不存在的 hazard。那個字在 `0x802BA660` 以上（`CPU-44` 記的 code／data 分界），**是資料被當成程式碼解**。🔴 **所以正確的說法不是「什麼都沒動」，而是：這棵樹裡唯一看得見這個修法的地方就是那個 load；**(c)** line 可組態成 16／32／64／128 位元組，所以 `w-line` 原本「STALE 跑到 +192 就作廢」會把一條真的 128 位元組 line 記成 void，探針延伸到 +320、門檻移到 +256；**(d)** `ICACHE` 是 direct-mapped 或 two-way、`DCACHE` 只有 direct-mapped —— **關聯度不再是「任何來源都沒有，哪裡都沒有」**，I 側是 {1,2}、D 側預測 direct-mapped，**量到 K = 4 就否證這條血緣**。③ 🔴 **`s-isc` 的控制位元定案：bit 6 與 bit 24**，來源是 LX4189 §3.4.1 的 `STATUS` 圖（27-23、21-16、7-6 全是「0」欄位，*"ignored on write and are 0 ASE），誠實的說法要照這句寫。🔴 **同一張圖把 bit 16 自己也放在「0」欄位裡**，所以裝置端期望值也不再是留白：**bit 16 讀回為 0**。④ **三個未定的 qemu 欄全部量掉**（`qemu/2026-08-26/probe3.txt`）：`CU3` 在 Malta 上不會黏、`s-isc` 三顆全 0、`TC0CNT` 每次讀 `FFFFFFFF` —— 🔴 walk，D 側那支由 cell A 在**執行期**閘控 —— A 若沒量到 stale line，D walk 每一格都回 fresh，而那與「沒有 D-cache」讀起來一樣。② 🔴🔴 **這顆上有一塊 16 KiB 的本機指令 scratchpad，而它和預測中的 I-cache 一樣大。** 這個 repo 從來沒有任何一列記過它。找到它的路是去問 `CCTL 裡**（逐位元組相同，md5 `623d85d7…`，**一票不是三票**）；`refs/` 自己那份資料表的「16Kbyte I-MEM, 8Kbyte D-MEM」；以及 🔴 **這台自己的 kernel `0x80002210`–`0x80002300`** —— 設 `CU3`、`0x020`、`0x202`、把 `0x002B8000`＋`0x3FFF`（**16 KiB**）寫進 `CP3 $0`／`$1`、發 `0x010`、再把 `0x002C0000`＋`0x1FFF`（**8 KiB**）寫進 `$4`／`$5`。**名字與行為互相解釋，而兩個尺寸與資料表的 I-MEM／D-MEM 逐一吻合。** **又是 `arch/rlx/` 那個失誤**：命名檔就在樹裡，而「還開著的路是一份會講的文件」這句話是在那份文件已經在磁碟上時寫的。③ 🔴 **`CCTL` 是 0→1 邊緣觸發的** —— 寫一個已經是 1 的位元不做任何事，所以「寫一次就期待有效果」的探針是一支**不會失敗的工具**。`rlx_cctl` 本來就 clear／write／clear，加上 `CPU-39` 量到讀側恆為 0，RLX4181 的 read-modify-write 形式在這顆上退化成普通寫入 —— 兩件事對上了。④ 🔴 **`CPU-25` 的來源盤點錯了，而且是往「來源更多」的方向錯。** `refs/RTL8196E-VEx-CG_Datasheet_1.1.pdf` 第一頁就寫 I 16 KiB／D 8 KiB，**而 `SOURCES.json:195` 從索引寫下來那天就逐字引著這句**。所以**「任何來源都沒有」的那一格是關聯度，不是 I-cache 大小**。⚠️ 資料表給尺寸但不給 line 大小也不給關聯度（對 11,467 行 grep 只命中交換器 MAC 表）。⑤ 🔴 **`docs/rlx-cache-and-cp0.md` §② 的「cell E 讓 cell A 可判讀」太強**：E 只在 write-back 那個分支裡是 A 的正控制；E 若量到 write-through，它對常駐與否一句話都沒說，**而剩下的「不 read-allocate」與「別名被 snoop」只有真的 bus master 分得開**。對 `R6` 它們等價，前提要等 `R6` 用真引擎重測。⑥ **量了一件本來以為要花一次上機的事**：`stage2.bin` 的**程式區一條 COP3 都沒有**（97 個 opcode `0x13` 命中全部 ≥ `0x8040A5B8` 且全是 ASCII），所以 loader 是對著 **reset 預設**的 BASE/TOP 發那兩個命令。⚠️ 那個零的控制比這個專案平常的弱：同一支掃描器在 kernel 裡找得到四條 `mtc3`，但那是**另一個檔案**。⑦ **`TC0CNT`（`0xB8003108`）成為一個新儀器，而它的極限先寫下來**：70 ns／LSB、9.9998 ms 回繞、模數不是 2 的冪 —— **單次存取永遠量不到，只能 loop-of-N**。速率 **14.286057 | **Last session but twenty-four** | 🔴 **2026-08-26，桌面，不通電 —— `R1g-5` 的寫作，`R1-gate` 結案，而寫作本身否證了這個 gate 倚賴的兩個說法。** ① **`docs/rlx-cache-and-cp0.md` 寫成**，四個下游決定各配上決定它的那個讀數：①`CCTL 0x002`，**而它只涵蓋指令側**；②**未答**；③`0x80000080` 可以；④timer 驅動是前置。② 🔴 **決定②寫成未答，而理由比進來時以為的更硬**：不只是裝置→CPU 一個字沒量，**CPU→裝置那一半對 descriptor ring 的樣式也沒涵蓋** —— `probe1` cell 1／cell 5 的 store 兩格都打在 **D-cache 沒有持有的 line** 上，所以 write-through 與「write-back 但不做 write-allocate」給出同一個讀數；而兩份 GPL drop 的 `boards/rtl8196e` 都寫 **`CONFIG_ARCH_CACHE_WBC=y`**（同源，一票不是兩票）。ring 的樣式是「先 load 再 store 同一個 descriptor」= 一次 **write hit**，正是六格裡沒有一格碰過的情況。③ 🔴 **`notes/cache-model.md` 的否證條件字面上成立了**：這台自己的 kernel 裡有 **37 條 MIPS-II `cache` 指令，全部是 D 側**，`0x8000CA40`–`0x8000CD4C`，op 欄只有 `0x11`／`0x15`／`0x19`、base 只有 `v0`／`a0`、offset 只有八個 stride `0x10`；另外 15 條是 data，位址全 ≥ `0x802BA660`，**三個彼此獨立的性質把 code 與 data 完全分開**。當初那次回 0 的掃描**只掃了 `stage2.bin`**。VMA base 有控制（`jal` 落在合理 prologue：正確 base 58.0 %，四個錯 base 0.2–3.0 %），掃描器有正控制（同一支在 `stage2.bin` 上重現五個已知 CCTL 位址**與**一個已知的 data 偽陽性）。⚠️ 這**不**代表它是 MIPS32 核心，`Config.M = 0` 是量的。④ 🔴 **CCTL 有名字了**：`arch/rlx/mm/cache-rlx.c`（Realtek，2008）明寫 `0x1 DInval`／`0x2 IInval`／`0x100 DWB`／`0x200 DWB_Inval`，而 **`0x100` 這個 repo 從來沒有一列記過它**。當初「沒有 Lexra 專屬 cache 檔」的結論是**只看了 `arch/mips/mm/`** —— 這顆走的是 `arch/rlx/`，而這個 repo 早就在別處引用過它。`0x010`／`0x020` **仍然無名**，但現在是**這台上兩個獨立實作**（loader 與 kernel）都在 reset 發它們。⑤ 🔴 **`SPEC.md` `CPU-19` 從寫下來那天起就沒被 `spec-check.py` 檢查過**：`` `Status.IsC\|SwC` `` 裡那個 `\|` 沒跳脫，七欄的表裡它有八格，C4／C5 把**來源欄**當擁有者欄、找不到路徑、把它算進「跳過」。**補了 C8 與第九個突變**，而 M9 就是把那個 `\|` 還原回去；順手把 §17 五列 3 格對 4 格的參差補齊。⑥ **`CPU-25` 第一次有一個切自這台的來源**（D 8 KiB／line 16 B），而三個數字的強度不一樣，寫進表裡了。**零 flash 位元組，零電源循環。** `docs/rlx-cache-and-cp0.md`、`notes/cache-model.md`、`SPEC.md` `CPU-19`／`CPU-20`–`CPU-24`／`CPU-25`／`CPU-43`–`CPU-45`、`tools/spec-check.py` |
| **Last session but twenty-five** | 🔴 **2026-08-25, at the bench — `R1g-4b`, the second seating. One power cycle, 23 captures, four prediction blocks, 🔄 **no flash-write command issued** *(was “zero flash bytes”; no `FLR` bracket ran)*, and `R1e` CLOSES.** `bench/2026-08-25b/`. **16 of 16 captures written before the block that predicted them; every one of the 16 byte counts exact** (`reply-size.py`, whose sweep over `bench/` now reads 135 modelled / 0 unexplained). ① 🔴 **`CPU-27` is measured: `Status = 1000FC00`, `BEV = 0`.** The vectors are in RAM at `0x80000080` **and the core fetches there** — `break` trapped into our handler and came back (`break.count=1`, `cause=00000024` = ExcCode 9, `break.epc=80500270`, the exact address of the `break` in the emitted image). 🆕 The withdrawn `1000FC01` was right about every bit **except `IEc`**, and `IEc=0` is what *`J` clears `IE`* predicts for a payload — vindicated with a mechanism rather than resurrected. ② 🔴 **`CPU-04`'s value is measured: `PRId = 0000CD01`**, and the prediction written into `PREDICTIONS-b4-block1.md` before the run hit exactly. **The value is 量 and the NAME is not** — nothing here maps `0xCD01` to `RLX4181` rather than `RLX5281`, so **do not write `RLX5281`**; what is missing is a source, not a measurement. ③ 🔴 **`F50b` is answered and the answer is the expensive one: `Count` is not implemented.** rd 9 reads `00000000` `S_ZERO`, `count.before = count.after = count.delta = 0`. **So `R5-0`'s SoC timer driver is a PREREQUISITE, not a bonus, and `R1c` loses its first timing route** — one of the four decisions this gate exists to make. ④ 🔴 **`nowrite = 0` across all 256 rows is what makes that zero real**: `mfc0` always writes `rt` on this core, so *the destination was never written* is excluded **by the instrument built to detect it**. Must-fix 4's question, answered. ⑤ 🔴 **The CP0 ignores the select field**: `moves = 8` and all eight are `rd 1`'s selects, `rows.suppressed = 0`, `rows.printed = 39`. R3000-class decode, measured. ⑥ 🔴 **`Random` (rd 1) is the positive control this census could not otherwise have** — sixteen values across its eight rows, index field **5 … 29, all inside 0 … 31**, which **corroborates `CPU-08`'s 32 TLB entries by a route with no TLB probe in it**. Without a register that *does* move on this die, *`Count` does not move* would have been unfalsifiable; qemu's `S_MOVES` is a control on qemu. ⑦ 🔴 **CP0 20 (`CCTL`) is separated at last**: `probe1`'s `XCT0` read `0` and could not tell *reads zero* from *destination not written*; with `nowrite=0` it can. **CP0 20 is a write-only command register that reads back zero** — the sentence `R5b`'s MTD driver needs. ⑧ **`Config` reads `00000000`** → `Config.M = 0`, **not a MIPS32 core**, proved outright — **and there is no `Config1`, so `CPU-25` cannot come out of CP0.** ⑨ **`traps = 0`** on all 256: an unimplemented CP0 read returns zero and does not trap. ⑩ **`rfe` balances the KU/IE stack** — `status_end == status` exactly, derived before the run from the R3000 shift rule. ⑪ **Two channels and every control fired**: all 40 header words identical, seal `EC84408D` agreeing, poison margin 8/8 `DEADC0DE` with un-poisoned DRAM beyond it, `H2h-utlb` byte-identical to **this seating's** `H0c`, `H2i-below` proving `probe2` wrote nothing below its block. 🔴 **And the false alarm the seating avoided is measured**: against 2026-08-25's `H0c` it *differs*, because the UTLB vector is DRAM bias and this DRAM's bias moves 4–27 bits per 256 across power cycles. ⑫ 🔴 **Five of my predictions were refuted and all five are kept**, the expensive one being the last: **a second `J 80500000` booted the vendor kernel**, because **the loader re-stages `0x80500000` on a watchdog reset too** — so a payload cannot be re-run on one power cycle without re-uploading, which nothing had written down. Cost: one power cycle; nothing earned was lost. ⑬ **Three tool defects, found by pointing the tools at the new captures**: `reply-size.py check` crashed on the one input it exists to report (`UNREADABLE` could never print) — 12 cases → **21**; `boot-timeline.py`'s artifact anchor assumed a one-byte prefix and read **4.2 ms** where tonight's two-byte prefix should give 342.5 — pooled spread 149.1 % → **2.5 %**, 12 cases → **15**; `exc.S`'s comment had `0x110F0000` for `0x10F00000`. `RUNSHEET.md` § Results B4 `R1g-4b`; `SPEC.md` `CPU-04`/`CPU-08`/`CPU-25`/`CPU-27`/`CPU-36`–`CPU-42`/`MEM-15`/`REG-13`/`LDR-22`/`CLK-15`; `bench/2026-08-25b/` |

| **Last session but thirty-three** | **2026-08-24, at the bench — seating 2 part two.** One power cycle, 🔄 **no flash-write command issued** *(was “zero flash bytes”; no `FLR` bracket ran)*, 33 captures, and the four headlines are a re-measurement, a closure, a refuted premise and three retractions. ① 🔴 **The timer base clock is `200.0049 MHz ± 0.0015` (±7 ppm)**, from four reads of the tick counter at `0x8040DCE8` across a **2,080-second** baseline with the intervals taken from `.log` mtimes — and `.timing` shows a reply's last byte arriving at line rate, so the offset from execution to mtime is fixed and cancels in a difference. **Every pair that excludes the shortest baseline gives `100.0018 Hz` to five significant figures.** So `CLK-02`'s 199.48 MHz and `CLK-04`'s 99.74 Hz are **superseded, and the 0.26 % they reported as a property of the board was the instrument**: seating 1 hand-timed 61.842 s, a human reading a clock is good to about ±0.15 s, and ±0.15 s in 61.8 s *is* ±0.25 %. The compiled-in `0x0BEBC200` is right to **24 ppm**, inside a normal crystal's tolerance; both old numbers stay in `RUNSHEET.md` `E2` with the reason they fell. ② 🔴 **`C-14` closes on a bit that moved rather than on an absence**: `PABCD_DAT` bit 5 `1`→`0` under the held button, `CNR` and `DIR` unchanged, no `Booting...` — a GPIO, not `RESET#`. **`D3` is retired before it was spent**, and `notes/power-and-programmer.md` §3's *"this unit's loader polls only the UART"* is refuted by the same three cells. ③ 🔴 **`0x81000000` is not scratch**: `C7-pre` read a live 32-byte-periodic descriptor table at `0x81000400` — `next = prev = &next` at `0x81000418`, and uninitialised DRAM cannot produce its own address — and `C7-pre2` a second live structure at `0x81800000` pointing to 28.04 MiB. **That refutes `RUNSHEET.md` §C's premise**, which argued from the address being far above the loader's image and the staged kernel: part one wrote onto a live structure and nothing broke, and that was luck. `§G`'s upload address moves to `0x80A00000`, chosen by a probe (`G4-addr-probe`) instead of by an argument, and `D2b` gains a third outcome. ④ **Three intermediate conclusions of mine were retracted, each by a cell that had named the outcome in advance**: *"`PSRP` bit 8 is sticky"* (by `E11c2`, which read a port whose jack was empty so no new down-event was possible — bit 8 went 1→0 in one read, and `NET-11` and the datasheet were right); *"`PSRP1` has no jack"* (by `E11e` — the jack count had been reported at the bench, not measured); *"`CONT`'s silence is residue in the line buffer"* (by `flush-cont`, which returned a bare prompt with no `Unknown command !`). `tools/check-predictions.py` is what makes *in advance* checkable at all — four prediction blocks, written `+8.2 s` … `+650.4 s` before the captures they name. ⑤ Free and unasked: `NET-13` closes with the physical jack order **`0, 2, 3, 4, 1`**, so a driver taking the port index from the silkscreen position is wrong on exactly one jack; `PSRP` bits 6 and 5 are the negotiated flow control (`ANLPAR` `0xC1E1` → `0xCDE1`, XOR `0x0C00`, and the `PSRP` pair moves with it and nothing else does); `0xB8000000` reads `0x8196E001`, the SoC naming itself while its own loader prints `chipName: UNKNOWN`; and a `DW` reply is structurally `len(cmd) + 2 + 47 × lines + 9` bytes, which predicted six reply sizes exactly, every one before its cell ran. `bench/2026-08-24b/`, `RUNSHEET.md` Results and Results — B2, `docs/loader-command-semantics.md` §f, `docs/loader-phy-and-switch.md` §7, `notes/power-and-programmer.md` §3, `bench/README.md` |
| **Last session but thirty-four** | **2026-08-24, desk, after the bench** — three tools, and the sharpest finding is about a control I had described to the operator as load-bearing. An adversarial review of `hazlint` 1.0 (four lenses, every claim put to a separate agent whose job was to refute it) returned **17 confirmed of 24 claimed**. ① 🔴 **`K4`, the population control, built its words with a private helper, so it exercised a code path no scanned file ever took** — it could not have caught any defect in span construction and it looked exactly like it could. It now goes through `raw_span()`, the same call `--raw` makes. ② The `lwl`/`lwr` pair exemption skipped the whole successor instead of the `rt` merge alone, so it swallowed the successor's **base** register — and since `reads()` did not model the merge either, **the exemption had only ever suppressed true positives**. ③ Six span defects: two sections of a `.o` both report `sh_addr == 0` and collided in the address map; a flat word list made the last word of one span the predecessor of the first word of the next; a span running past EOF was silently shortened. ④ Scan coverage: a section whose `sh_offset` is past EOF vanished from the scan *and* the report; only `PF_X` segments were scanned with nothing saying what was not. ⑤ Decoder: the four REGIMM branch-likely forms and the coprocessor branches were not recognised as branches at all. **`1474 / 646 / 0` is unchanged through all of it.** `hazlint` is 1.1; the suite went 42→56 with a mutation per fix, because the review's finding 6 was that the old suite could not tell the patched tool from the shipped one. Also: `tools/rlxprobe/` segment 0 (23 cases) with `hazlint` wired in as a build **gate**, and `console-capture.py --esc-after` (7→10) which unblocks `§D`. `SPEC.md` `TC-06`/`TC-07` |
| **Last session but thirty-five** | **2026-08-24, at the bench** — seating 2 part one, and **three of the four unplanned findings are about the measuring system rather than the silicon**. ① **The console line buffer is 128 bytes** (device: exactly 128 ESC bytes then `Unknown command !`, seven times; code: `memset(buf,0,128)` + `readline(buf,128,1)`), **and `readline` writes its NUL only on the CR path** — so a line of exactly 128 characters is unterminated and the tokeniser scans past the buffer into the saved registers. **`C7` was going to send 173 characters, which `readline` cuts at exactly 128, with `EW` as the command.** `C7` is rewritten to 12 values / 119 characters and no command line may ever be 128. It also answers `C7`'s own question: one line carries 12 words, so a 1 KiB probe needs 22 lines not 15 — R1's no-network path is 47% dearer. ② **`A2` is closed by changing the instrument, not the cell** — `console-capture.py --esc` streams ESC from inside the capture loop, so the pre-prompt stream is kept. ③ **`A0` is a new cell** and it measured that re-opening the serial port does not reset the board (`BRD-09`'s DTR inference → measurement); its first attempt failed on queued ESC, which `SPEC.md` `LDR-16` already knew — **the rule was in the table and not in the procedure, so it was rediscovered rather than followed**, and it is now in the procedure. ④ `EW` rounds an unaligned address **up**, `EB` does not; one loader, two write primitives, opposite handling. ⑤ **`§D` was stopped before it ran**: `D1`+`D2`+`D2b` need one capture that sends a command *then* streams ESC across the reboot, and `--esc` runs before `--send`. Third cell lost to an instrument that could not do it (`A2`, `E5`) and the first caught in advance. `SPEC.md` `LDR-06b`–`06d`, `LDR-08`, `LDR-09`, `LDR-16`, `LDR-23b`, `REG-23`; `LOG.md`; `bench/2026-08-24/` |
| **Last session but thirty-six** | 2026-08-24, desk only. **`hazlint` (`DAY-ZERO` item 7) is written and its three acceptance numbers hold**: `stage2.bin` → **1,474 loads / 646 with a `nop` / 0 violations**, exit 0; upstream's broken `P9-12` payload → **2 violations at `0x1c` and `0x2c`**, exit 1; the population count removed three different ways → exit 2 every time. Eight controls inside the tool, 42 cases outside it in `tools/test-hazlint.sh`, of which nine mutate the tool and demand it notice. **Three corrections came out of building it**, all in `plan/DAY-ZERO.md` §7 and `docs/loader-command-semantics.md` §9: ① the plan named the **156-byte** payload as the negative control and the broken one is **148 bytes** — the 156-byte file is the *fixed* one and would have reported 0 violations, i.e. a negative control that could not fire; both are now kept, 148 as the negative control and 156 as a differential positive control, and the pair differs by exactly two `nop`s; ② the broken payload has **two** violations, not the one the post-mortem named — `lbu a0` then `beqz a0` at `0x1c` was never written up, and the fix repaired both; ③ **`-march=mips1` does not compile on this host** — `cc1: error: '-march=mips1' requires '-mfp32'` — so the `rlxprobe` build line in items 6 and 8 was unbuildable as written; `-msoft-float` fixes it and is the honest flag for a core with no FPU. Also: §9's rodata figure of **28** is withdrawn as a control (three classifiers give 445 / 236 / 829 and none gives 28; the tool that produced it never named its watch list) and replaced by a property that can fail. `SPEC.md` `CPU-14`, `CPU-18`, `CPU-29`–`CPU-31`, `TC-06`, `TC-07` |
| **Last session but thirty-seven** | 2026-08-24, desk only, **two sessions in parallel on the same working tree**. ① **`SPEC.md`** — one table of every number this project holds about the device, each row marking where the *value* came from separately from where its *name* came from; an index that owns nothing, with §17 listing every blank beside the experiment that fills it. `CLAUDE.md` gained the rule that a session which produces, changes or refutes a number changes `SPEC.md` in the same commit. ② **the sheet that was about to be run had three defects and now has five new cells.** `D1`'s stopwatch cannot measure what it was aimed at (164 µs / 2.30 ms, both under any instrument here) — **but the longest `OVSEL` setting can** (84.1 ms / 1.177 s, 14× apart), so `D4` arms it by hand and `D1` is its control. `D2` gets the whole word `A5100000` and got stronger: **the loader never writes `WDTCNR` except at two `sw zero`-then-spin sites**, so `B7`'s `A5000000` is a hardware reset default and `D2`'s path has no software in it. **`AUTOBURN 0` had never been shown to work** — `C6`. `tools/console-capture.py` written, 7/7 with five controls, three of which failed first time. Session `B3` (R0) written. ③ **`tools/spec-check.py`** — seven checks over `SPEC.md`, eight mutations, and a control counts only if it produces a finding the file did not already have. It found 28 defects on its first run and none was a false positive: eleven rows carried a datasheet value under a provenance mark meaning *nothing* (the mark alphabet was missing 文, read from a document), ten pointed at the file that owns the semantics instead of the one that owns the reading, and **`0x80000000` turned out to be owned by no file in this repository**. Two of its own controls were false: one tested an invariant that had just been downgraded, the other deleted the wrong row. `CLAUDE.md` no longer restates the active gate, and `refs/README.md` no longer claims a tool that does not exist enforces the two-source rule. ④ **every tracked program is now recorded `100755`** — seven of the first ten had drifted to `100644` because DrvFs hides mode changes from git, and `tools/test-file-modes.sh` reads the index in both directions to keep it that way. `LOG.md` |

## Carried forward

Open questions that outlive a single session. Each one names the gate that will
close it. **An item with no owning gate is a bug in this list.**

| # | Question | Owning gate |
|---|---|---|
| `TMR-1` 🆕 | 🔴 **`rtl819x-timer` 的 `tc1_ext_trusted` 會對掉了整圈的資料宣稱可信,而那正是一個信任旗標最不該做的事。** 量 2026-09-03(桌面,由 `CLK-17` 導出):`rtl819x_ext_advance()` 算 `d = (now − last) & MASK`,只在間隔小於一個週期(**9.395016 s**)時才是真的;旗標的條件是 `d < MASK>>1` = **4.697508 s**。超過一個週期之後餘數 **alias 回可信帶內**:30 s 的真實間隔是 428,581,710 counts(3 圈),算出的 `d` 是 **25,928,526**,`trusted` 讀 **1**;60 s 是 6 圈,`d` = 51,857,052,一樣讀 **1**。**旗標的契約在它自己的定義域內是對的,而沒有任何東西強制那個定義域** —— 這就是缺陷。修法很便宜:在 `rtl819x_ext_advance` 裡一併記下 `jiffies`,若 `Δjiffies × TICK_NSEC` 超過一個週期就把 `trusted` 打成 0。🔴 **不能在 `R5-2` 修**:`config/` 是 `RECIPE_ID` 的來源,動它就作廢已經 stage 的 `rlxfw-r51-20260903.bin`。`R5-2` 的卡片把「`trusted` 會讀 1 而且是假的」寫成預測 `Q5`,所以這個缺陷會在矽片上被確認而不是只停在紙上 🟢 **2026-09-03（seating 11）：在矽片上確認了，機制完全正確，而門檻錯 71.43 倍。** 因為 Linux 底下 `CDBR` 是除 1000 不是除 14（`REG-11`），一個週期是 **671.07 s** 不是 9.395016 s，所以卡片預測的 30 s／60 s 別名**沒有發生**。量，`TM-5b-arm`→`TM-5b2`，跨過那個門檻：真實 gap **140,693,532** counts，`tc1_ext_gap_max` 讀 **6,475,672**（正好 = 該值 mod `2^27`），`tc1_ext_trusted` 讀 **1** —— 掉了整整一個週期 134,217,728 counts 而旗標宣稱可信。🔴 **而 seating 11 找到第二個缺陷，這一列原本沒有它，它也不是別名。** 讀 `rtl819x-timer.c:476-477`：`rtl819x_ext_advance()` 的**唯一**呼叫點在 `rtl819x_tc_read_proc` 裡且由 `armed` 守著，**所以 `tc1_ext` 是「有人去 `cat` 的那些區間的總和」，不是計數器的展開** —— 沒有讀者時它就靜靜停住。量，`TM-5c`：462 秒沒人讀 `/proc`，`tc1_cycles=98,840,142` 對 `tc1_ext=6,477,776`，差 92,362,366，`gap_max` 沒動，`trusted` 還是 1。**兩個修法都是 `R5-3` 的**：由保證每個週期至少跑一次的東西來推進，以及讓旗標在 `Δjiffies × TICK_NSEC` 超過一個週期時拒絕。 | **`R5-3`**(第一個會重建映像的計時器步驟)。`SPEC.md` `CLK-22`、`notes/timer-driver.md` §5.2.1 ① |
| `LEDGER-2` 🆕 | 🔴 **`ledgerscan check` 比對的是「路徑集合」,從來不看深度欄,所以一列可以少宣告深度而閘門一路綠。** 量 2026-09-03:`check` 在 32 條 in-scope 路徑上報 ok,同一時間 `scan --domain timer` 把 `kernel/time/jiffies.c` 標成 **`line`**,而帳本那一列宣告的是 **`name`** —— `notes/timer-driver.md:273` 引的是 `kernel/time/jiffies.c:37`。**深度正是「看過介面」與「讀過程式碼」的分界,而那是整個帳本存在的理由**;現在唯一在比對它的是一個人把兩份輸出並排看。列已就地更正,**檢查器沒有修**。⚠️ 修法不是把 `scan` 的深度硬塞給 `check`:`scan` 的深度是全 repo 觀察到的最大值,而帳本的深度是「這一列說它拿了什麼」,兩者相等只是通常成立;要做的是讓 `check` 在兩者不一致時**報告**而不是**判定** | **`R5-9`**(`docs/driver-diff.md` —— 那是帳本的深度第一次真的決定一列的分數)。`docs/blind-write-ledger.md` §7 ⑧ |
| `CI-5` 🆕 | 🔴 **CI 的 wall-clock 不是 suite 成本的訊號，而這一次是量到的而不是提醒。** 量 2026-09-03，`gh run view --json jobs` 逐 job 逐 step 拆三次執行：`33595236873`（09-02 05:34）wall 14:32、`instruments` exec 9:20、其中 `apt` **0:24**；`33657418793`（09-02 16:50）wall 9:35、exec 9:21、`apt` **0:24**；🔴 `33657603686`（09-02 16:52，**與前一次相隔 118 秒**）wall **19:59**、exec **19:43**、`apt` **10:50**。**`apt` 26 倍，這個 repo 一個字都沒改**；suite 那一側逐項不動（`test-console-capture-mutants` 三次都是 6:01）。`census` 的 `queued=19:49` 是它 `needs:` 別人，是結構不是延遲。⚠️ 上一段開場就是把 14m32s 讀成「suite 慢了五分鐘」（實際是 9m20s ＋ 五分鐘排隊），所以這一列存在的目的不是「以後小心」，是**把那個警告換成一個有數字的讀數**。**兩個可做的（都還沒做）**：① 在 `.github/workflows/ci.yml` 的 `apt` 步驟上加 cache 或 `--no-install-recommends`，把最大的一項從 10 分鐘壓回半分鐘；② 或者不要壓，但**在任何寫上去的地方都不要引用 run 的 wall-clock**，只引用逐 step 的數字。🔴 **先做②，因為①會改 CI 而 CI 現在是綠的，而②不花任何東西**。🔄 **2026-09-03（第二十九段）：兩次新的 run，五個資料點，而第五個抓到這一列自己用錯的量。** 這一列（與那一段的第一版寫上去）都拿 `instruments` 的 **exec** 當「suite 側」的代表 —— **而 `exec` 是所有 step 的和，`apt` 在裡面**，所以 `33657603686` 的 1183 s 之所以是 1183，正是因為它的 `apt` 是 650 s；它不能同時當「suite 側穩定」的證據。**承載這個宣稱的量是 `exec − apt`。** 量，五次三天：**536／537／533／533／523 s**（`apt` 24／24／650／29／35），全距 14 s ≈ **±1.3 %**。🟢 **結論沒有倒，緊度從 ±0.4 % 收到 ±1.3 %。** 🆕 **而今天兩次相隔 29 分鐘、母體只差兩個散文檔的 run 逐 step 拆開，是比逐 job 更強的證據**：每一支 suite 都在 ±5 s 內（`test-spec-check-mutants` −5、`test-console-capture-mutants` −3），**而 `apt` 自己 +6** —— 逐 job 只能說「總和穩定」，逐 step 能說「每一支都沒動」，而這一列要的是第二句。**所以 ② 要收窄**：不只是「不要引用 wall-clock」，是**不要引用任何含 `apt` 的合計**，包括 `exec` | **`R5` 的準備** |
| `HAZ-1` 🆕 | 🔴 **`hazlint-objs` 的母體是 `arch/rlx`，而 `R5` 的六支驅動一支都不在裡面。** 量 2026-09-03：`R5-1` 的產物是 `drivers/clocksource/rtl819x-timer.o`，`tools/hazlint-objs.py --tree` 走的是 `find -L <top>/linux-2.6.30/arch/rlx -name '*.o'`，所以 `P2` 會對一個**不含任何驅動的母體**回報 0 個違規，而那個 0 讀起來會像是「驅動也掃過了」。今天是直接跑 `tools/hazlint <obj>` 補上的（100 個 load、0 違規、`K2`／`K4`／`K4b` 都發射），**但那是一次手動動作，不是一個會重跑的檢查**。⚠️ 這跟 `CI-3` 是同一類但不同層：`CI-3` 是「一支工具從沒宣告過」，這一列是「一支工具宣告了、跑了、而它的母體選得比它的主張窄」。**做法**：`hazlint-objs` 多一個 `--also DIR`（可重複），把 `drivers/clocksource/` 這種目錄加進 sweep；正控制是**移掉那個目錄必須讓被掃到的物件數下降**，而不是「還是綠的」。⚠️ **`R5` 每加一支驅動，這一列的漏掃就多一份** | **`R5` 的準備 —— 在 `R5-4` 之前** |
| `MARK-1` 🆕 | ⚠️ **`rlxfw-marks verify` 讀的是 mark 列，而 `MK`／`MK2` 這種 build 列會把一整個檔案帶進映像，`verify` 對它一個字都不說。** 量 2026-09-03：`MK2` 的 insert 是 `obj-y += rtl819x-timer.o`，沒有自己的字串，所以 `verify` 那句「present exactly once in mine, absent from the vendor's」涵蓋 12 個 mark、**不涵蓋 642 行的驅動**。今天是用三個手動證據補的：`vmlinux` 裡 `rtl819x-tc1` 出現一次、**2026-09-02 建的 `i3`／`r44a` 裡出現零次**（一個**早於這個檔案存在**的控制，比事後重建的對照更硬）、以及 `System.map` 上的六個符號含 `__initcall_rtl819x_timer_init3`。🔴 **`check` 也不夠**：`check` 讀樹，它只會說 Makefile 那一行在不在；一個編得出來卻沒被連進去的驅動兩個檢查都是綠的 —— 這正是 `rlxfw-marks.py` 自己的抬頭在講 `check` vs `verify` 的那件事，而 build 列把它又打開了一次。**做法**：build 列多一個可選欄位，宣告「這一列帶進來的檔案必須在映像裡留下的字串」（驅動用 clocksource 的 `.name` 就好），`verify` 照 mark 列一樣查一次；正控制是**改掉那個字串必須紅** | **`R5` 的準備 —— 在 `R5-4` 之前** |
| `LOOP-3` 🆕 | 🔴 **一個只能跑一次的迴圈不是迴圈,而 `R5` 是六支驅動。** 量 2026-09-02(讀,在板子旁邊定案而沒有花掉那次電源):`looprun --iterations N` 只是把 `loop_once` 叫 N 次,而 `loop_once` 從 `S4` = `J BFC00000` 開始 —— 那是 **loader** 命令,第一輪結束時 kernel 在跑、loader 不在了。而且它會**更早**死在一個更糟的理由上:每個擷取階段的 `--out` 由 `--cell` 導出、沒有輪次索引,`console-capture.py` 又拒絕覆寫,所以第二輪死在「輸出檔已存在」,**在真正的原因之前**。一個死在檔名上的執行對迴圈一無所言。**今天做的是讓它拒絕**(`M8`,以及負控制 `M8b`),不是讓它會跑 —— 要會跑需要「從 Linux 回到 loader 的路」加上「每輪一組輸出名」,那是設計決定不是一個旗標 | **`R5` 的準備** |

| `ESC-1` 🆕 | ⚠️ **The reset latency is bounded above and cannot be resolved further with the terminator this card used.** 量 2026-09-01: `boot-timeline`'s `entry` over 21 resets is 1.7–2.8 ms, mean 2.4 ms — but those captures ran `--esc-period 0.002`, whose `drain()` forces the read cadence to a 2.088–2.126 ms median, so `entry` and *one read period* are not separable. The chain itself resolves **0.216 ms** (pooled over 108,527 inter-read gaps, 5,618 of them under 1 ms), so this is the ESC loop's pacing and not the hardware's floor. **What it does establish**: latency < 2.8 ms, consistent with the derived 2.184 ms, and the rival 1118 ms model refuted by ~400×. The experiment that would resolve it costs nothing extra — `--esc-period 0.0005` on one reset cell  🔄 **2026-09-02:實驗寫成卡片了,還沒跑。**`bench/2026-09-02/PREDICTIONS-B8-block7.md` 的 `LP-e5`／`LP-e5b` 兩格用 `--esc-period 0.0005`,而**兩格是因為 n=1 什麼都量不到**。`Q4` 的否證條件寫著:讀節奏若仍 ≥ 2 ms,那 `--esc-period` 就不是設定它的東西,這一列的模型就是錯的 🟢 **CLOSED 2026-09-02(seating 10),而收掉它的是一個形狀不是一個界線。** 量:節奏 **0.666／0.664 ms**(要求 0.0005),`entry` **2.3／2.4 ms**;**同一次電源循環**裡粗網格的 `LP-rz`(節奏 2.156 ms)也是 **2.3 ms**。`entry` 的下界就是一個讀取週期,所以在 2.1 ms 節奏下 2.3 ms 是 **1.1 個週期**(貼在地板上),在 0.666 ms 節奏下同一個值是 **3.5 個週期**(離開地板)。**尺細了 3.2 倍、數字沒動**,所以 2.3–2.4 ms 是板子上的沉默。⚠️ **它是逾時的上界不是逾時**(橫跨逾時＋重置＋到第一個開機位元組),推的 2.184 ms 落在底下,相容而未證實。🔴 **而這一列自己的一句話被否證了**:它寫「`--esc-period` 調到 0.0005,節奏就降到 ~0.5 ms」 —— 拿到的是 0.666。超額是**加法**的不是乘法的,n=4:實際減要求 = 140／156／166／164 µs,而拿粗的那一對配的乘法模型預測細的那一對只有 35 µs。**約 150 µs／次的地板,所以任何寫 `--esc-period X` 再拿 X 推論的格子推的是儀器不產生的數字** —— 要讀 `.meta.json` 的 `achieved_period_s`。`SPEC.md` `CLK-14` | **`R4-3`** ✅ |

The accepted candidate is left in the global `0x8040DD3C`. The plan's row saying it was still open was stale. **The rlxfw answer is different from the vendor answer**: since `EB`/`EW` write any address unbounded, R4 puts an **uncompressed cmdline buffer at a fixed address in its own image** and patches it from the console before `J`. | C-19 | **What made the console adapter leave the host's USB bus mid-session?** **量** 2026-08-24 at 04:33:57: `dmesg` recorded `vhci_hcd: connection closed` → `release socket` → `usb 1-1: USB disconnect` → `cp210x ttyUSB0 ... disconnected`; on the Windows side `usbipd list` moved the busid out of *Connected* entirely and `Get-PnpDevice -PresentOnly` found no `VID_10C4`, **so it left the host bus and not merely the usbip attachment**, and WSL itself did not restart. **The board and the operator are both cleared by the timeline**: the previous capture landed 7 min 24 s earlier and the gap is pure idle, and after re-attaching the tick counter read 428,675 where a board that had reset during the outage would have read at most ~60,000. Candidates, none settled: a usbip socket transient, USB selective suspend failing to resume, a loose connector. **If it recurs after a long idle, selective suspend is the one to test.** It matters because R4's `bench-ci` runs unattended across exactly that kind of idle; a second finding came free with it and is not this question — the first command sent after a re-enumeration is echoed but not acted on (`CONT`, `flush-cont`, `CONT2`) | R4 |

Every time the plan changes, one line here, and one row in the plan's §0. **A plan
that records where it was wrong is more credible than one that looks right.**
